- [sceAudioOut2MasteringInit](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAudioOut/src/AudioOut2Mastering.cpp) (libSceAudioOut) - flag values unknown; only 0 is accepted. Whether the console reports an error for a repeated `sceAudioOut2MasteringInit` or for `sceAudioOut2MasteringTerm` without a matching initialization is unknown; both return 0. PPSA01289 creates the audio device of its next world before it destroys the previous one, so it calls Init twice before Term twice, and only logs a non-zero result
- [sceAudioOutSetMixLevelPadSpk](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAudioOut/src/AudioOut.cpp) (libSceAudioOut) - effect of a negative mix level unknown; only 0 to 32768 (0 dB) is accepted
- [sceAudioOutSetMixLevelPadSpk](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAudioOut/src/AudioOut.cpp) (libSceAudioOut) - no source applies the level (shadPS4 only stores it); it is assumed to be a linear gain on the port's own output, and the -9 dB default (11626) is applied to every pad speaker port, including those of titles that never call the function
- [sceAudioOutGetLastOutputTime](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAudioOut/src/AudioOut.cpp) (libSceAudioOut) - signature and behaviour taken from the [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4) and [fpPS4](https://github.com/red-prig/fpPS4) reimplementations of the PS4 library, not confirmed on a PS5 title: the time is the process time at which `sceAudioOutOutput` or `sceAudioOutOutputs` accepted the port's last block, not the time the block reached the device
- [sceAudioOutSysClose, sceAudioOutSysConfigureOutput, sceAudioOutSysOpen](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAudioOut/src/AudioOut.cpp) (libSceAudioOut) - imported by PPSA99051 and PPSA99997, which fail to load without them; added without an implementation and declared without parameters. [EVO-PLAYER-PS5](https://github.com/sainsaji/EVO-PLAYER-PS5/blob/da0c0451333bab82e79fffd2de4718e917f9dcb0/projects/evoplayer/core/src/audio/evo_pt.cpp#L166-L240), whose audio code both titles share, calls `sceAudioOutSysOpen`, `sceAudioOutSysConfigureOutput` and `sceAudioOutSysClose` only to open a port for HDMI bitstream passthrough. Its declared arguments are not confirmed on a console. Audio reaches the host device through SDL as PCM, so there is no HDMI output to switch
- [sceAudioOut2PortCreate](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAudioOut/src/AudioOut2Port.cpp) (libSceAudioOut) - MAIN (type 0) format validation follows PS5 firmware 13.60 measurements: bits 8-11 accept 1, 2, 8 or 12, bits 0-6 accept 0 or 1, bit 7 requires 8 channels, and bits 12-31 do not affect validation. The sample types are still read as float and int16; bit 7's layout, the PCM effect of bits 12-31 and the port `flags` are unverified. Other port types retain unverified format validation, including 6 channels and rejection of bits 12-31
- [sceAudioOut2PortCreate](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAudioOut/src/AudioOut2Port.cpp) (libSceAudioOut) - 12 channels are assumed to be L R C LFE Ls Rs Lb Rb Ltf Rtf Ltb Rtb as in KytyPS5's 12-to-8 fold, and 6 and 8 channels to be its first 6 and 8; no title or SDK header confirms the order
- [sceAudioOut2PortCreate](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAudioOut/src/AudioOut2Port.cpp) (libSceAudioOut) - the stereo fold is assumed, not the console's downmix: C at -3 dB into both sides, Ls/Rs, Lb/Rb and Ltf/Rtf at -3 dB and Ltb/Rtb at -6 dB into their own side, LFE dropped
- [sceAudioOut2PortSetAttributes](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAudioOut/src/AudioOut2Port.cpp) (libSceAudioOut) - each PCM buffer set (attribute 0) is taken as one grain: buffers set between two pushes are queued and played one per grain, at most 16 queued. A grain with no new buffer repeats the last one only when the port has always been given that same buffer (rewritten in place); a port whose buffers move on plays silence instead. Inferred from PPSA12544, whose object port (type 6) is given one grain of a 64-grain ring per set, often two or three between pushes and then none for a few; the console behaviour for several sets per push and for a push without a new buffer is not documented
- [sceAudioOut2PortGetState](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAudioOut/src/AudioOut2Port.cpp) and [sceAudioOut2GetSpeakerInfo](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAudioOut/src/AudioOut2SpeakerArray.cpp) (libSceAudioOut) - the port state reports the channel count of the port's data format, output main, volume 127 and a zero reroute counter and flags; the speaker info reports a stereo front pair at -30 and +30 degrees. Taking the channel count from the data format follows [SharpEmu](https://github.com/sharpemu/sharpemu/blob/main/src/SharpEmu.Libs/Audio/AudioOut2Exports.cs), another emulator, and no console measurement exists for either call: the output of a pad port, the volume after `sceAudioOut2PortSetAttributes` and the reroute counter on a device change are unverified, as are the speaker types other than stereo. ASTRO BOT and ASTRO's PLAYROOM reportedly stop on an assertion that depends on these values (#4317), but which field matters has not been isolated
- [scePsmlMfsrGetContextBufferRequirement1100, scePsmlMfsrCreateContext1100, scePsmlMfsrGetDispatchMfsrPacket1100](../../core/libs/prx/libScePsml_debug/Export.cpp) (libScePsml_debug) - argument layouts unknown; only 0x8A810001 (not initialized) is returned
- [scePsmlMfsrGetDispatchMfsrPacket1000](../../core/libs/prx/libScePsml_debug/Export.cpp) (libScePsml) - unknown signature, imported by PPSA25344; declared without parameters. libScePsml and libScePsml_debug are built from the same source
- [scePsmlMfsrCreateContext1300, scePsmlMfsrGetDispatchMfsrPacket1300](../../core/libs/prx/libScePsml_debug/Export.cpp) (libScePsml) - unknown signatures, imported by PPSA28416 next to `scePsmlMfsrGetContextBufferRequirement1100`; declared without parameters. The names are not in the aerolib NID list; they hash to the imported NIDs `kLF7WtQ1-Dg` and `DDVUyPyYHTA` (`tools/nid_names.py`)
- [scePngEncEncode](../../core/libs/prx/libScePngEnc/Export.cpp) (libScePngEnc) - whether rows may stay unfiltered when `filter_type` names a subset of the filters is unknown; as in shadPS4, they may only for 0 and for the all-filters mask
- [sceJpegEncEncode](../../core/libs/prx/libSceJpegEnc/Export.cpp) (libSceJpegEnc) - `restart_interval` follows SharpProspero's field doc (0 none, -1 every row of blocks, positive a block count); a positive value is taken as the JPEG DRI interval in MCUs and -1 as one restart per MCU row, which no reference confirms. Other negative values throw
- [sceVoiceQoSInit](../../core/libs/prx/libSceVoiceQoS/Export.cpp) (libSceVoiceQoS) - `0x804E0905` for a second initialization comes from [prosper](https://github.com/mattias800/prosper)'s reading of the module and was not measured: on a PS5 a valid call blocks inside a homebrew payload, so it never returns there. The app type and the block are not used, as no endpoint is implemented
- AudioIn and NpSessionSignaling exports added without an implementation have assumed signatures
- [sceNpSessionSignalingGetConnectionFromPeerAddress](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNpSessionSignaling/Export.cpp) (libSceNpSessionSignaling) - imported by PPSA01289 and added without an implementation; unknown signature
- [vieBRwlh1Lw](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgc/Unimplemented.cpp) (libSceAgc) - unknown name, signature
- [scePadIsRemoteController](../../core/libs/prx/libScePad/Export.cpp) (libScePad) - the name hashes to NID `fCWdlnmB1Ks`; the signature `(int handle, bool* remote)` comes from PPSA16064, which zeroes a byte, passes its address after `scePadGetControllerInformation` and ignores the result. The error codes follow the other pad calls. Controllers are local devices, so the answer is always false
- [scePadSetAngularVelocityBiasCorrectionState](../../core/libs/prx/libScePad/Export.cpp) (libScePad) - inferred signature `(int handle, bool enabled)` follows `scePadSetTiltCorrectionState` and `scePadSetAngularVelocityDeadbandState`; an invalid handle returns `PAD_ERROR_INVALID_HANDLE`, disabling is a no-op, and enabling throws because bias correction is not modelled
- [sceKernelGetOperationMode](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/System/src/Process.cpp) (libkernel) - the signature `(int* mode, int* submode)` comes from PPSA12544, which logs both values after the call; the values are unknown and 0 is reported for both, as in the [prosper](https://github.com/mattias800/prosper/pull/4153) reimplementation. Null outputs throw
- Font, Http2, Net, Ssl, SystemService and libkernel exports imported by PPSA12544 and added without an implementation have unknown signatures
- [sceHttp2GetMemoryPoolStats, sceSslGetMemoryPoolStats, sceNetGetMemoryPoolStats](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNet/Export.cpp) (libSceHttp2, libSceSsl, libSceNet) - the signatures `(int id, stats*)`, the layouts (pool size, peak and current use, and for Http2 and Ssl a 32-bit reserved field) and the error codes were measured on a PS5 (firmware 13.60). The pool size is the one given at init or pool creation; nothing is allocated from these pools, so the peak and current use are 0, where the console already reports use right after initialization (0x15C0 bytes for Ssl, 0x1640 for Http2 and 4 for a Net pool)
- [sceNpWebApi2GetMemoryPoolStats](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNpWebApi2/Export.cpp) (libSceNpWebApi2) - imported by PPSA21703's Unity PSN plugin. The signature `(int libCtxId, stats*)` and the layout of the Http2 statistics above (measured on a PS5 for Http2, Ssl and Net) are assumed for this library, not measured. The pool size is the one given to `sceNpWebApi2Initialize`, and the peak and current use are 0. The error codes are unknown, so a null output or an unknown context throws
- [sceSslGetCaList, sceSslFreeCaList, sceSslUnloadCert](../../core/libs/prx/libSceSsl/Export.cpp) (libSceSsl) - signatures and the 16-byte `SceSslCaList` layout (an array of certificate handles and a 32-bit count) follow [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero/blob/5dfdb4b0b166e83dc9ae275001afb695f12c7174/src/SharpProspero/Interop/Net/Http.cs) and shadPS4's `OrbisSslCaList`, not confirmed for the PS5. On the console the list holds the certificate authorities the SSL service has; [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/pull/4104) returns a placeholder certificate from `sceSslGetCaCerts` because Rise of the Tomb Raider fails its initialization checks without one. No root certificate bundle is shipped and no loaded certificate is kept, so here a context holds none: `sceSslGetCaList` clears the list and returns `NOT_FOUND`, as `sceSslGetCaCerts` does, `sceSslFreeCaList` accepts only that cleared list, and `sceSslUnloadCert` (imported by PPSA15508) has nothing to drop and is assumed to return 0. A null list returns `0x8095177A`, which `sceSslGetCaCerts` and [KytyPS5](https://github.com/KytyPS5/KytyPS5/blob/59183dbfa81ed7efdff6069177fed50de0552725/src/libs/network.cpp) return for a null argument (shadPS4, PS4, returns `0x8095F007`). An unknown context throws (KytyPS5 returns `SSL_ERROR_INVALID_ID`, `0x8095F006`, from `sceSslGetCaCerts`), and so does a list `sceSslGetCaList` did not return
- [sceHttp2SetResolveRetry](../../core/libs/prx/libSceHttp2/Export.cpp) (libSceHttp2) - signature `(int id, int32_t retry)` from libSceHttp's `sceHttpSetResolveRetry`; the value is accepted and ignored like the other Http2 settings, since no request reaches the network
- [sceHttp2WebSocketCreateRequest, sceHttp2WebSocketCloseAsync, sceHttp2WebSocketSendTextMessageAsync, sceHttp2WebSocketSendDataMessageAsync](../../core/libs/prx/libSceHttp2/Export.cpp) (libSceHttp2) - imported by PPSA16064 and not called during its start-up; unknown signatures
- The Share, NpTrophy2, NpEntitlementAccess and WebBrowserDialog exports imported by PPSA12544's Unity plugins and added without an implementation have unknown signatures
- [sceNetShowNetstat](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNet/Export.cpp) (libSceNet) - imported by PPSA11386 and not called during its start-up; unknown signature, declared without parameters
- [sceAjmDecWVorbisCreateHeaderPacket, sceAjmDecWVorbisCreateSetupPacket](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAjm.native/Export.cpp) (libSceAjmWVorbis, implemented in libSceAjm.native) - unknown signatures
- Acm, GameLiveStreaming, Net, NpEntitlementAccess, NpWebApi2, VoiceQoS, WebBrowserDialog and libkernel exports imported by PPSA14632 and added without an implementation are declared without parameters
- [libSceAudioPropagation](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAudioPropagation/Export.cpp) (libSceAudioPropagation) - signatures and struct descriptors recovered from PPSA21567's calls (#481), not from documentation. The system options layout is unknown (only checked for non-null); the fourth argument of `sceAudioPropagationSourceCalculateAudioPaths` is unknown; `sceAudioPropagationSourceGetAudioPath` is assumed to output an 8-byte path handle. The meaning of the `RenderInfo` word at +0x28 is unknown (PPSA21567 and PPSA21564 pass 2); only 2 is accepted. Both titles pass one `RenderInfo` per call: a count of 0 throws, and larger counts are handled element by element without proof. No error code is known, so invalid input throws instead of returning one
- [NGS2 mastering voice gain](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNgs2.native/src/Voice.cpp) (libSceNgs2.native) - the param id 0x30000004 is taken from PPSA22520 and the layout from [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4)'s `OrbisNgs2MasteringVoiceGainParam`; no reference gives the units or range. The levels are assumed to be linear factors applied when the mastering voice is mixed into the render buffer, so the voice's own samples stay unscaled; the LFE level applies to channel 3 of 6- and 8-channel voices, assuming the L R C LFE order of the AudioOut2 entries above, and the full-band level to the rest. Negative levels are accepted, and a new setup resets both levels to 1
- [NGS2 stereo mastering voice into a surround buffer](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNgs2.native/src/Render.cpp) (libSceNgs2.native) - PPSA21402 renders a 2-channel mastering voice into a 6- or 8-channel render buffer, which the mixing stage rejected. The console's conversion is unknown: the left and right channels are assumed to go to the first two buffer channels (front left and right) and the remaining channels stay silent, with no centre, LFE or surround content. Any other channel count mismatch still throws
- [NGS2 voices without a format](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNgs2.native/src/Voice.cpp) (libSceNgs2.native) - a sampler voice whose setup has no format (all zero) accepts an add of no waveform blocks without `APPEND`, which only applies `RESET` and the `CONTINUE` state, and matrix levels with no levels, which clear that matrix. PPSA36591 sets such a voice up, adds no blocks and sets no levels when a button is pressed at its title screen. Whether the console returns success or an error there is not measured; blocks with data, `APPEND` and an empty matrix on a voice with a format are still refused
- [NGS2 silent waveform blocks](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNgs2.native/src/Voice.cpp) (libSceNgs2.native): flag `0x10` (named `SILENCE` here; no public reference names it) lets a waveform block declare samples without data. PPSA04867 adds one block with flags `0x11`, `data_size` 0 and `num_samples` 16385, then another with flags `0x11` and a fully specified 0x800-byte, 1024-sample mono block, which takes the ordinary path. A dataless block under the flag outputs `num_samples` frames of silence, reports them through `decoded_data_size`, and finishes like a complete block; a dataless block without the flag still throws as not fitting its data. The flag's real name, its interaction with ATRAC9 and repeats, and the console's `waveform_data` for silent blocks are unverified on hardware
- [NGS2 streaming PCM waveforms](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNgs2.native/src/Voice.cpp) (libSceNgs2.native) - PPSA21402 streams its music by adding one block whose `num_samples` is the whole track and whose `data_size` is a 16 KiB first piece, with `CONTINUE`, and then adds each following piece with flag `0x2` (called `APPEND` here) and the same `num_samples`. No reference describes flag `0x2`. A 16-bit PCM block without skipped samples or repeats whose `num_samples` exceed its data is assumed to start such a stream; flag `0x2` is assumed to append the block's data to the last streaming block, ignoring its `num_samples`; the voice is assumed to output silence without advancing while it has played all appended data and `CONTINUE` is set; and the waveform is assumed to end at `num_samples` or, once `CONTINUE` is cleared, at the end of the appended data. `sceNgs2VoiceGetState` reports the bytes played as `decoded_data_size`, which the title uses to release its queued data, and the read position as `waveform_data`. Appending to a block that is not a stream, or other formats, skipped samples, repeats and partial frames, throw
- [NGS2 matrix levels](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNgs2.native/src/Voice.cpp) (libSceNgs2.native) - levels are clamped to -4..4 and NaN is kept, as the PS4 firmware 11.00 `libSceNgs2.sprx` stores them; not yet confirmed on the PS5 library. That library rejects a level count of 0 and keeps the matrix; the PS5 error code is unknown, so a count of 0 throws, except on a sampler voice without a format, where it clears the matrix (see NGS2 voices without a format). `sceNgs2VoiceRunCommands` is not in that library, so its matrix levels command is assumed to clamp the same way
- [NGS2 sampler voice state flag EMPTY](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNgs2.native/src/Render.cpp) (libSceNgs2.native) - a playing sampler voice that still accepts blocks (`CONTINUE`) and has played all queued blocks reports `INUSE|PLAYING|EMPTY` (0x20) and keeps playing, so blocks added later play on. PPSA18391 (engine: Clickteam Fusion) reads `state_flags & 0x22 != 2` from `sceNgs2VoiceGetState` as the end of a sound and only then reuses the voice. When the console sets and clears EMPTY, and whether it also reports it for other voice types, is unverified on hardware.
- [sceAvPlayerStartEx](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAvPlayer/Export.cpp) (libSceAvPlayer) - start info layout unknown; it is ignored and playback starts as with `sceAvPlayerStart`
- [sceAvPlayerInit / sceAvPlayerInitEx](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAvPlayer/src/Player.cpp) (libSceAvPlayer) - behaviour without a memory replacement unknown; frame and sample buffers then come from the guest heap
- [sceAvPlayerInitEx](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAvPlayer/Export.cpp) (libSceAvPlayer) - the PS5 init data layout (`this_size` 0x230: auto start at 0x74, nine 0x30-byte thread parameter blocks from 0x78, output video frame buffer count at 0x228) follows [KytyPS5](https://github.com/KytyPS5/KytyPS5/blob/main/src/libs/avPlayer.cpp), [prosper](https://github.com/mattias800/prosper/blob/main/prosper/src/hle/video/avplayer.cpp), [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero/blob/main/src/SharpProspero/Interop/Media/AvPlayer.cs) and [sharpemu](https://github.com/sharpemu/sharpemu/blob/main/src/SharpEmu.Libs/AvPlayer/AvPlayerExports.cs), which agree on it. PPSA25063 fills the same fields, writes auto start 0 and starts playback with `sceAvPlayerStart`; a title that sets auto start has not been seen. A smaller `this_size` is read with the PS4 offsets. The thread parameters are ignored
- [AvPlayer auto start](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAvPlayer/src/Player.cpp) (libSceAvPlayer) - the auto start that `sceAvPlayerAddSource` queues for a player with auto start or without an event callback starts the player only while it is still ready: a title that starts the player itself before the auto start runs keeps its playback and the frames it holds. Not confirmed on a title.
- [SceAvPlayerVideoEx](../../core/libs/SceTypes.hpp) (libSceAvPlayer) - frame rate field and encoding unknown; it is left zero in frame and stream info
- [sceRazorCpuFlushOccurred](../../core/libs/prx/libSceRazorCpu/Export.cpp) (libSceRazorCpu) - signature and return values (1 = flush since the last call, 0 = none) from [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero); without a capture nothing is flushed, so it returns 0 and writes 0 cycles to the optional out pointer (whether the real library writes it when nothing was flushed is unknown)
- [sceRazorCpuPushMarkerStatic, sceRazorCpuPopMarker](../../core/libs/prx/libSceRazorCpu/Export.cpp) (libSceRazorCpu) - signatures from PPSA26344's calls; (name, color, flags) assumed for the push; both do nothing without a capture
- [sceRazorCpuPushMarker](../../core/libs/prx/libSceRazorCpu/Export.cpp) (libSceRazorCpu) - signature (label, color, flags) from [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero), the same as sceRazorCpuPushMarkerStatic's except that the label is copied; it does nothing without a capture and returns 0
- [sceRazorCpuPlotValue, sceRazorCpuWriteBookmark](../../core/libs/prx/libSceRazorCpu/Export.cpp) (libSceRazorCpu) - signatures (series, float value) and (label, optional description) from [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero), matching PPSA06320's calls; both do nothing without a capture and return 0
- [sceAjmBatchJobGetInfo](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAjm.native/src/Ajm.cpp) (libSceAjm.native) - writes the result and format sideband of a run job without buffers with the `SIDEBAND_FORMAT` flag (0x400000000000), as [KytyPS5](https://github.com/KytyPS5/KytyPS5) does, not confirmed on a PS5 title; the format reports bitrate 0 for ATRAC9 as in KytyPS5, while [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4) derives it from the superframe size; before the instance is initialized it returns the not-initialized result (1) and no format, like the other run jobs here, while KytyPS5 returns result 0 with the format of the uninitialized decoder
- [sceAjmDecMp3ParseFrame](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAjm.native/src/Ajm.cpp) (libSceAjm.native) - signature, result layout and rate tables taken from the [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4) reimplementation of the PS4 library, not confirmed on a PS5 title: the layer field is not checked and MPEG-2.5 stops at 64 kbps; the original file length (`parse_ofl`) layout (Xing/Info + LAME tag, VBRI, Fraunhofer `0xB4` block with its CRC, encoder delay = samples per frame + LAME delay + 529) also comes from shadPS4 only, and the Fraunhofer block has no other public description
- [NGS2 port matrix](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNgs2.native/src/Render.cpp) (libSceNgs2.native) - a port without a matrix, or with a selected matrix whose levels were never set, mixes through a default channel map: mono reaches both stereo channels at -3 dB and the 5.1 and 7.1 centre, stereo folds to mono at -3 dB per side, and wider sources fold down with 0.5 and -3 dB gains. The map and the equivalence of an unset matrix with no matrix were measured from the PS4 firmware 11.00 `libSceNgs2.sprx`, not yet from the PS5 library. That library only accepts submixer and mastering voices with 1, 2, 6 or 8 channels, so the map into 3, 4, 5 or 7 channels throws. A matrix set with fewer levels than the destination needs still mixes only the rows it has
- [sceAjmBatchJobGetCodecInfo, sceAjmBatchJobGetGaplessDecode](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAjm.native/src/Ajm.cpp) (libSceAjm.native) - the `RUN_GET_CODEC_INFO` flag (0x800), the ATRAC9 codec info layout and its next frame size are taken from the [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4) and [RPCSX](https://github.com/RPCSX/rpcsx) reimplementations, not confirmed on a PS5 title; a run job without buffers returns result 0, as in shadPS4. The gapless sideband reports the total and skip counts the title set and does not count them down: RPCSX does the same, shadPS4 counts them down and BryKytyPS5 returns its current counts
- [AJM Opus decoder](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAjm.native/src/Ajm.cpp) (libSceAjm.native, codec 24) - the initialize parameter layout (`u32 channels, u32 sample rate, u32`, seen as `2, 48000, 0`) and the little-endian `u16` byte count before each packet come from one title (The Smurfs Dreams); only 48000 Hz and a zero third word are accepted
- [sceAjmBatchJobSetResampleParametersEx](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAjm.native/src/Ajm.cpp) (libSceAjm.native) - the `(info, instance, float ratio_start, float ratio_change_per_sample, flags, result)` signature is inferred from the name and from `sceAjmBatchJobSetResampleParameters`; with a zero change per sample it sets the ratio as `sceAjmBatchJobSetResampleParameters` does (Watch Dogs: Legion, a title, calls it so). How a non-zero change ramps the ratio is not confirmed on a console (no source: KytyPS5 ignores it, prosper stubs it, the PS5 firmware does not export the batch builders to a payload): it is assumed to be added to the ratio after each emitted output frame, clamped at zero, and kept across batches. a title passes one call with a change of 4.6e-7, so the exact rule matters little there.
- [sceAjmBatchJobControl](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAjm.native/src/Ajm.cpp) (libSceAjm.native) - RESET (bit 13), INITIALIZE (bit 14) and the sideband input order (gapless decode, then initialize parameters) come from [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4) (PS4); other flags throw
- [sceAjmBatchJobControl](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAjm.native/src/Ajm.cpp) (libSceAjm.native) - RESET is assumed to clear the context as sceAjmBatchJobClearContext does
- [sceAjmBatchJobControl](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAjm.native/src/Ajm.cpp) (libSceAjm.native) - initialize parameters are 8 bytes for ATRAC9 and M4AAC, 12 for Opus and none for MP3, as one title passes them; other codecs and sizes, and an output other than the 8-byte result, throw
- [sceAjmBatchJobControl](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAjm.native/src/Ajm.cpp) (libSceAjm.native) - SIDEBAND_GAPLESS_DECODE with RESET overwrites both counts as in [RPCSX](https://github.com/RPCSX/rpcsx); shadPS4 ignores a zero count when the other is nonzero. Without RESET they differ, so it throws
- [NGS2 custom submixer rack](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNgs2.native/src/Custom.cpp) (libSceNgs2.native, rack 0x4002) - structure layouts from [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4)'s `ngs2_custom.h`; module id 0x1f for UserFx2 and 0x1d for UserFx, voice parameter ids `0x40001f00 | module index` and `0x40001d00 | module index`, the process flags (1 on the first process call after setup, 2 after a parameter change, 0 otherwise) and the call order (setup per voice at rack creation, process after the inputs are mixed, cleanup at destruction) come only from [KytyPS5](https://github.com/KytyPS5/KytyPS5) and shadPS4. Only UserFx and UserFx2 modules on a single buffer are accepted: other module ids, more buffers, a module control handler, different input and output channel counts, setup flags and default rack options throw. Each module's state is a separate buffer, not the `state_offset` slice of the voice state, and `sceNgs2VoiceGetState` still throws for this rack. As in KytyPS5, UserFx2 process runs only on grains where an input produced samples, so an effect tail stops with the input and a pending flag 2 waits for the next audible grain
- [NGS2 reverb rack](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNgs2.native/src/Reverb.cpp) (libSceNgs2.native, rack 0x2001) - follows the PS4 firmware 11.00 `libSceNgs2.sprx`, read and checked against its output at 11.025 to 48 kHz, including parameter changes during playback (differences at float rounding level); not yet compared with the PS5 library. Structure layouts come from [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4)'s `ngs2_reverb.h` and the param ids 0x20010000 (setup) and 0x20010001 (I3DL2) from PPSA22520. Only the default `reverb_size` of 1 is handled, with its 14400-sample reflection buffer; other sizes and the library's half-rate mode throw. System sample rates below about 7.5 kHz, where an allpass delay would have no samples, throw. Setup only accepts equal input and output channel counts
- [sceHttpParseResponseHeader](../../core/libs/prx/libSceHttp/Export.cpp) (libSceHttp) - signature follows [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero/blob/main/src/SharpProspero/Interop/Net/Http.cs); parsing and error codes follow [shadPS4's reverse-engineered PS4 library](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/blob/main/src/core/libraries/network/http.cpp), not confirmed on a PS5 title or hardware. Folded values retain their line breaks and whitespace; an empty first line starts the value scan after its line feed. A consumed byte count above `INT_MAX` throws instead of wrapping.
- [sceHttpParseStatusLine](../../core/libs/prx/libSceHttp/src/Parse.cpp) (libSceHttp) - behaviour and error codes follow the [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4) reimplementation of the PS4 library, not confirmed on a PS5 title; unlike it, no byte past `lineLen` is read
- [sceHttpUriMerge](../../core/libs/prx/libSceHttp/src/Uri.cpp) (libSceHttp) - signature follows [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero/blob/main/src/SharpProspero/Interop/Net/Http.cs); validation, size formula, absolute/relative split and path join follow the disassembly-like body in [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/blob/main/src/core/libraries/network/http.cpp), not confirmed on a PS5 title or hardware. The base URL goes through our `sceHttpUriParse`, which removes dot segments from the base path, while the relative part is appended unswept; base query and fragment are dropped. The relative reference is only classified by its scheme and a leading `//`, and only references with `//` are validated; as in shadPS4, `//host/x` is returned unchanged instead of taking the base scheme, a reference with a scheme but no `//` (`mailto:x`) is joined onto the base directory, and `?q` and `#f` are appended to the base directory instead of the base path.
- [sceHttpUriUnescape](../../core/libs/prx/libSceHttp/src/Uri.cpp) (libSceHttp) - signature follows [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero/blob/main/src/SharpProspero/Interop/Net/Http.cs); percent decoding, invalid sequences and error codes follow [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/blob/main/src/core/libraries/network/http.cpp), not confirmed on a PS5 title or hardware. Size includes the final NUL; malformed escapes and `+` remain unchanged, and decoding runs once.
- [sceHttpUriSweepPath](../../core/libs/prx/libSceHttp/src/Uri.cpp) (libSceHttp) - behaviour follows the [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/blob/main/src/core/libraries/network/http.cpp) reimplementation of the PS4 library and its tests (including the PS4 SDK example), not confirmed on a PS5 title or hardware. It is not RFC 3986 5.2.4: a path that doesn't start with `/` is copied unchanged and a trailing `.` or `..` without a slash is kept. Unlike shadPS4, nothing at or past `srcSize - 1` is read, and `srcSize == 1` writes only the terminator
- [sceHttpSetResponseHeaderMaxSize, sceHttpRedirectCacheFlush, sceHttpsUnloadCert](../../core/libs/prx/libSceHttp/Export.cpp) (libSceHttp) - signatures follow [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/blob/main/src/core/libraries/network/http.cpp) (PS4); no PS5 reference has them
- [sceNpSetTimeout](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNpManager/Export.cpp) (libSceNpManager) - the signature follows [orbital](https://github.com/AlexAltea/orbital/blob/b321fcdbd700cc02da1cb1ad25412781d5ec73c6/tools/ida/db_types.json), [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/blob/0fe263a4760dfbfa973366890061749b4af0de97/src/core/libraries/np/np_manager.cpp) and [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero/blob/5dfdb4b0b166e83dc9ae275001afb695f12c7174/src/SharpProspero/Interop/Np/NpAccount.cs); the rejected values (all five 0, negative retries, a resolve timeout under 1 s or another timeout under 10 s, 0 meaning the default) are shadPS4's, not checked on a console. An id that was never created or was deleted returns `REQUEST_NOT_FOUND`, as in shadPS4; there is no network, so the timeouts are not stored
- [sceNpGetUserIdByAccountId](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNpManager/Export.cpp) - the `(account id, user id output)` signature and its errors follow [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/blob/main/src/core/libraries/np/np_manager.cpp): account id 0 or a null output returns `SCE_NP_ERROR_INVALID_ARGUMENT`, and a signed-out NP returns `SCE_NP_ERROR_SIGNED_OUT` without writing the output. NP is always signed out here, so no account id resolves to a user
- [sceHttpSetRequestStatusCallback](../../core/libs/prx/libSceHttp/Export.cpp) - the three-argument ABI follows [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/blob/main/src/core/libraries/network/http.cpp). Registrations and user data are retained and removed on request deletion; the offline backend never dispatches callbacks. Invalid ids return `0x80431100`, an uncited assumption, not a verified console error. Handle hierarchy, inheritance and deleted-handle validation remain incomplete; callback statuses and dispatch timing are unknown.
- [sceHttpSetRedirectCallback, sceHttpSetCookieRecvCallback, sceHttpsSetSslVersion, sceHttpsGetSslError](../../core/libs/prx/libSceHttp/Export.cpp) (libSceHttp) - signatures follow [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/blob/main/src/core/libraries/network/http.cpp) (PS4), which only logs and returns 0; a null `errNum` or `detail` in `sceHttpsGetSslError` is assumed to return `SCE_HTTP_ERROR_INVALID_VALUE` like `sceHttpGetLastErrno`
- [sceHttpSetAuthInfoCallback](../../core/libs/prx/libSceHttp/Export.cpp) (libSceHttp) - signature and callback type follow [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/blob/main/src/core/libraries/network/http.h) (PS4); no PS5 reference has them. The `id` is not checked, as in the other libSceHttp setters
- [libSceHttp2 cookie boxes](../../core/libs/prx/libSceHttp2/Export.cpp) (libSceHttp2) - `sceHttp2CreateCookieBox` is taken to take the library context id first, as in the [prosper](https://github.com/mattias800/prosper) reimplementation; further arguments are unknown and ignored. `sceHttp2SetRequestNoContentLength` is assumed to take only the request id. Cookies are never stored, so `sceHttp2CookieFlush` has nothing to discard
- [sceUserServiceGetUserName](../../core/libs/prx/libSceUserService/Export.cpp) (libSceUserService) - `0x80960105`, which a PS5 (firmware 13.60) returns for a user id it does not know, has no known name; it is `USER_SERVICE_ERROR_UNKNOWN_USER` here. On the console a registered user who is not logged in still gets their name; here only the initial user exists
- [sceNpSessionSignalingGetMemoryInfo](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNpSessionSignaling/Export.cpp) (libSceNpSessionSignaling) - unknown signature
- [sceNpSessionSignalingGetConnectionStatistics](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNpSessionSignaling/Export.cpp) (libSceNpSessionSignaling) - unknown signature
- [sceUserServiceGetAgeLevel](../../core/libs/prx/libSceUserService/Export.cpp) (libSceUserService) - on a PS5 (firmware 13.60) the value is the age set in the user's parental restriction (0, 7, 12 and 15 were measured) and `0xFFFFFFFF` without one; a user who is not logged in returns `0x80960009` with or without an output. Parental restrictions are not modelled, so the initial user is always reported as unrestricted. PPSA12544 (Unity) only stores the value for its user profile
- [sceUserServiceGetAccessibilityChatTranscription, PressAndHoldDelay, TriggerEffect, Vibration, ZoomEnabled, ZoomFollowFocus](../../core/libs/prx/libSceUserService/Export.cpp) (libSceUserService) - report 0, 0, 1, 1, 0 and 1, what a PS5 (firmware 13.60) reports for a user whose accessibility settings are all at their defaults; the console reports the user's own settings (a changed vibration intensity read 3), which are not modelled. What each number means is unknown. A user who is not logged in returns `0x80960009` with or without an output
- [sceGameUpdateGetAddcontLatestVersion](../../core/libs/prx/libSceGameUpdate/Export.cpp) (libSceGameUpdate) - a null `entitlement_label` throws: KytyPS5 accepts it, prosper returns `0x80412803`. Bytes after `found` are zeroed as in KytyPS5; prosper leaves them untouched
- [sceSystemServiceParamGetString](../../core/libs/prx/libSceSystemService/Export.cpp) (libSceSystemService) - the system name (parameter 6) is the fixed `PS5`, not the console's name; other parameters and buffers of 1 to 64 bytes throw
- [sceSystemServiceParamGetInt](../../core/libs/prx/libSceSystemService/Export.cpp) (libSceSystemService) - console language numbers 0 to 29 follow the PS4 SDK enum in [OpenOrbis](https://github.com/OpenOrbis/OpenOrbis-PS4-Toolchain/blob/06fcf7fd108fa080035cdd3259ed22eb07e0508c/include/orbis/_types/sys_service.h#L54-L83) (21 Arabic). 30 (Ukrainian) is not in it and follows PPSA99505 ([Jelly5](https://github.com/02dnot/Jelly5/blob/552b866d89b2a58b27f7f7b0484dc743ce235526/app/src/app/i18n.cpp#L29-L60)) and PPSA99002 ([ProsperoLight](https://github.com/blackbearreloaded/ProsperoLight/blob/b708bbde3457bef1073a9ab6959957cb691e8e2f/include/i18n.hpp#L252-L309)), whose numbers agree with the enum. Not confirmed on a console, and neither title starts on `main` yet. Parameter 400 returns the same number as parameter 1: PPSA01325 asks for 400 first, asks for 1 when that fails, and uses either answer as an index into the same 31 languages; in the PS5 10.01 and 12.60 `libSceSystemService` modules parameter 400 reads the same registry value as parameter 1, without parameter 1's mapping of newer language numbers by SDK version, and returns 0x80a10003 when an imported check that is not identified returns 1; the 4.03 module has no parameter 400. Neither the mapping nor that error is modelled, and the parameter's name is not known. Without `ANYPS5_LANGUAGE` the language is English (United States); the date format, time format, time zone, summer time and enter button stay fixed
- [sceNgs2ReportRegisterHandler, sceNgs2ReportUnregisterHandler](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNgs2.native/src/Unimplemented.cpp) (libSceNgs2.native, NIDs `uBIN24Tv2MI`, `nPzb7Ly-VjE`) are throwing stubs. The signatures (report type, handler, user data, out handle) and (report handle) follow [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero) and [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4)'s `ngs2_report.h`. No report is produced yet, so registering a handler cannot be honoured. PPSA04540 imports both and registers a CPU load handler (type 5)
- [sceNgs2SystemSetSampleRate, sceNgs2SystemSetUserData, sceNgs2SystemGetUserData, sceNgs2SystemLock, sceNgs2SystemUnlock](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNgs2.native/src/System.cpp) (libSceNgs2.native) - shadPS4 only checks the system handle (`INVALID_SYSTEM_HANDLE`) and SharpProspero names what each call does; neither gives the other limits. A sample rate of 0 and a null user data pointer throw, any other rate is accepted, and changing the rate while a sampler filter is enabled throws because the filter coefficients were computed for the old rate. Lock and unlock take the same lock as `sceNgs2RackLock`
- [sceNgs2RackGetInfo](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNgs2.native/src/Rack.cpp) (libSceNgs2.native) - field order from [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4)'s `OrbisNgs2RackInfo` with the 64-byte name of the other info structures (216 bytes); `type` is assumed to be the rack id divided by 0x1000, `min_grain_samples` 64 (the system minimum) and `state_flags` 1; `uid` comes from a per-process rack counter, `render_count` is the owning system's render count and `active_voice_count` counts voices that are not empty (paused and stopped voices included). The last process ratio and tick and `active_channel_work_count` are left zero
- [sceNgs2VoiceQueryInfo](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNgs2.native/src/Voice.cpp) (libSceNgs2.native) - only info id 0x4001 is known: PPSA22520 passes an 8-byte buffer and branches on the first word being 1 or 2, so it is assumed to be the voice channel count (0 before the voice is set up); the second word is zeroed and other ids throw
- [sceNgs2VoiceControl patch](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNgs2.native/src/Voice.cpp) (libSceNgs2.native) - a destination rack handle with input id 0 resolves to that rack's first voice; this matches a title's call that patches to a rack handle, but selection for racks with multiple voices has not been checked against a console. Nonzero destination input ids still throw. `sceNgs2VoiceGetPortInfo` reports the resolved voice handle and zero delay.
- [sceNgs2ParseWaveformFile](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNgs2.native/src/Atrac9.cpp) (libSceNgs2.native) - the signature `(path, offset, info)` is taken from the call site of PPSA21402, which sets only three argument registers; the file is read from `offset` to its end, the data and block offsets are taken to be relative to the start of the file, not of `offset`, and an unreadable file throws. The audio unit fields of a 16-bit PCM waveform are assumed to be one sample frame per unit and per frame; `INVALID_WAVEFORM_DATA` for a `data` chunk that runs past the buffer or ends inside a frame is assumed, and looped PCM and other PCM formats throw
- [sceSaveDataDirNameSearch](../../core/libs/prx/libSceSaveData.native/Export.cpp) (libSceSaveData.native) - the sort keys (0 directory name, 1 user parameter, 2 blocks, 3 modification time, 5 free blocks) and orders (0 ascending, 1 descending) follow shadPS4, not a hardware run; saves with equal keys keep directory-name order before a descending reverse. A save's size is the `blocks` of the `sceSaveDataMount3` call that created it, kept beside the save in `<dir>.blocks`; a save without a kept size takes that of its next mount that names one, and otherwise reports `SAVE_DATA_BLOCKS_MAX`. The search infos (blocks, free blocks and 32 reserved bytes per returned name, following shadPS4's `OrbisSaveDataSearchInfo`), `sceSaveDataGetMountInfo` and the two block keys use that size and, as free, what the save's files leave of it, each file rounded up to 32 KiB blocks; the console's file system overhead is not known and not counted. Keys other than 0, 1, 2, 3 and 5 (including 4) and orders above 1 return `SCE_SAVE_DATA_ERROR_PARAMETER`; shadPS4 and KytyPS5 only reject keys above 5, so key 4 is rejected here because no source names it. In the directory name pattern `%` matches any run of characters, none included, and `_` exactly one, as the comments of shadPS4's matcher describe; also not confirmed on hardware
- [sceSaveDataInitialize3](../../core/libs/prx/libSceSaveData.native/Export.cpp) (libSceSaveData.native) - a repeated initialize succeeds: PPSA12544's executable and its Unity SaveData plugin both initialize, and the plugin fails on any error. Initializations are counted and `sceSaveDataTerminate` ends the session at the last one; how the console pairs them is unknown
- [sceTextToSpeech2GetSystemStatus](../../core/libs/prx/libSceTextToSpeech2/Export.cpp) (libSceTextToSpeech2) - unknown signature; returns `EOPNOTSUPP` like `sceTextToSpeech2Initialize`
- [sceCoredumpAttachUserMemoryFile](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/System/src/Coredump.cpp) (libkernel) - unknown signature
- [sceCoredumpAttachMemoryRegion](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/System/src/Coredump.cpp) (libkernel) - unknown signature
- [sceKernelAddTimerEvent](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Equeue/Equeue.cpp) (libkernel) - `usec` is taken as the period in microseconds; re-adding an existing timer keeps its pending expiration count and restarts the period from the time of the call, where FreeBSD 12+ `filt_timertouch` clears pending expirations on `EV_ADD`; `usec == 0` fires on every wait. None of this is confirmed on a PS5 title
- [sceKernelWaitEqueue](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Equeue/Equeue.cpp) (libkernel) - a level-triggered user event (`sceKernelAddUserEvent`) is reported at most once per call, as in FreeBSD `kqueue_scan` (`sys/kern/kern_event.c`), which re-queues a reported knote without `EV_CLEAR` or `EV_DISPATCH` behind the scan marker, and kevent(2), where several triggers do not produce several kevents. Not measured on a console. Events are scanned in list order and the list is not rotated, so when `num` is smaller than the number of ready level events the later ones can starve, where FreeBSD moves a reported level knote to the tail. Several completions of one AMPR or VideoOut event within one call are returned as separate entries, where FreeBSD would aggregate them; the Orbis behaviour is unconfirmed
- [EVFILT_VNODE on macOS](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Equeue/src/FileEvent.cpp) (libkernel) registers `EVFILT_VNODE` on a duplicate of the descriptor in a host kqueue, whose notes have FreeBSD's values, and drops the `NOTE_LINK` that XNU adds to `NOTE_DELETE` when the watched file or directory is removed, since FreeBSD's `vop_remove_post` and `vop_rmdir_post` report `NOTE_DELETE` alone. As on Windows, a directory registered with `NOTE_EXTEND` is accepted but never receives it: XNU reports a move between directories as `NOTE_WRITE` alone, with `NOTE_LINK` for a subdirectory
- [sceRtcFormatRFC2822, sceRtcFormatRFC2822LocalTime, sceRtcFormatRFC3339LocalTime](../../core/libs/prx/libSceRtc/Export.cpp) (libSceRtc) - only shadPS4 (PS4) has bodies. A null tick returns `INVALID_POINTER` like the existing `sceRtcFormatRFC3339`, where shadPS4 formats the current time instead; offsets outside ±1439 minutes return `INVALID_VALUE` like `sceRtcFormatRFC3339`; the local-time variants use the host's offset at the given instant (shadPS4 uses `sceKernelGettimezone`, the current offset)
- [memcpy_s and memmove_s](../../core/libs/prx/libc/src/Strings.cpp) (libc) use the C11 K.3.7.1.1 and K.3.7.1.2 size constraints, with RSIZE_MAX taken as SIZE_MAX >> 1 as in memset_s. An oversized destination size returns ERANGE (34) without touching the destination; the console's RSIZE_MAX and exact error value are unverified. Constraint violations return an error without invoking the registered constraint handler, as before.
- [snwprintf_s](../../core/libs/prx/libc/src/FormattingWide.cpp) (libc) - follows C11 K.3.9.1.3 with `RSIZE_MAX` assumed to be `SIZE_MAX >> 1`. A runtime-constraint violation only returns a negative value: `set_constraint_handler_s` is not exported and the console's default handler is unknown. An invalid multibyte `%s` argument is copied byte by byte instead of being reported as an encoding error, as in `vswprintf`
- [fegetenv](../../core/libs/prx/libc/src/Math.cpp) (libSceLibcInternal, implemented in libc, NID `psx0YiAKm7k`) follows FreeBSD 11 amd64 [fenv.c](https://github.com/freebsd/freebsd-src/blob/4554bee35405b36ea19e9e60d6abe6ea534e36e0/lib/msun/amd64/fenv.c) with the 32-byte `fenv_t` of [x86/fenv.h](https://github.com/freebsd/freebsd-src/blob/4554bee35405b36ea19e9e60d6abe6ea534e36e0/lib/msun/x86/fenv.h): `fnstenv` fills the 28-byte x87 part, MXCSR follows at 0x1c, and the x87 control word is reloaded because `fnstenv` masks every x87 exception. That the console's `libSceLibcInternal` has the same code and layout is assumed, not read from its binary. A null pointer throws instead of faulting
- [swprintf_s and vswprintf_s](../../core/libs/prx/libc/src/FormattingWide.cpp) (libc) - follow C11 K.3.9.1.4 with `RSIZE_MAX` assumed to be `SIZE_MAX >> 1`: the complete output, including the terminating null, must fit in the buffer, otherwise it is a runtime-constraint violation that sets `buffer[0]` to zero and returns a negative value, and the buffer is not partially written. Argument and encoding checks match `snwprintf_s`
- [vsnwprintf_s](../../core/libs/prx/libc/src/FormattingWide.cpp) (libc) - `va_list` counterpart of `snwprintf_s` (C11 K.3.9.1.3): truncates to the buffer and returns the full length, with the same constraints and argument checks
- [vsnprintf_s and vsscanf_s](../../core/libs/prx/libc/src/Formatting.cpp) (libc) - `vsnprintf_s` forwards to `vsnprintf` semantics: the C11 K.3.7.1.5 runtime constraints (null buffer, `size == 0`, `%n`, null `%s`) are not checked, so `size == 0` returns the would-be length instead of a negative value, and no constraint handler is invoked. `vsscanf_s` returns the count of items converted before an undersized `%s`/`%c`/`%[` destination (0 when it is the first), not EOF. Neither is confirmed on a PS5 title
- [strndup and strdup](../../core/libs/prx/libc/src/AllocatingStrings.cpp) (libc) return null with `errno` set to `EINVAL` (22) when `source` is null, preventing a host segfault on invalid guest input. POSIX.1-2008 and FreeBSD 11.4 libc leave `strndup(NULL)` and `strdup(NULL)` undefined (faulting); exact console behavior on hardware is unverified.
- [libSceUlt ulthreads](../../core/libs/prx/libSceUlt/Export.cpp) - `_sceUltUlthreadRuntimeOptParamInitialize`, `_sceUltUlthreadRuntimeCreate` and `_sceUltUlthreadCreate` are assumed to take the same arguments as the existing exports without the underscore, including the trailing build version. `sceUltUlthreadTryJoin` is assumed to return `ULT_ERROR_BUSY` while the ulthread runs, and `sceUltUlthreadRuntimeDestroy` to return `ULT_ERROR_BUSY` while an ulthread created on the runtime is not joined. Neither error has been confirmed on PS5 hardware
- [libSceUlt queues](../../core/libs/prx/libSceUlt/Export.cpp) - the added `sceUltQueuePop`, `sceUltQueueTryPush`, `sceUltQueueDestroy` and `sceUltQueueDataResourcePoolDestroy` signatures are inferred from the existing push/pop and object APIs. Queue destruction is assumed to reject blocked callers with `ULT_ERROR_BUSY` and otherwise discard queued data; pool exhaustion returns `ULT_ERROR_AGAIN`. These rules, alignment checks and shared slot limits have not been confirmed on PS5 hardware or a title. Non-null queue and data-pool option parameters throw because their layouts are unknown.
- [localeconv](../../core/libs/prx/libc/src/LocaleSupport.cpp) (libc, re-exported by libSceAmpr) - the 0x90-byte Dinkumware struct (empty monetary strings, `CHAR_MAX` for the 14 `char` fields, `decimal_point` ".", `_False`/`_True` "false"/"true") is read from the `libc.prx` that Astro's Playroom ships. It is a fixed C-locale table: whether the console changes it with `setlocale` is not known, and `_Frac_grouping`, `_Frac_sep`, `_No`, `_Yes` are empty strings as in that dump
- [set_constraint_handler_s](../../core/libs/prx/libc/src/WideConversion.cpp) (libc, re-exported by libSceAmpr) - the default handler is assumed to abort; each module keeps its own handler state, so a handler set through one library is not seen by the other. `ctime_s` and `asctime_s` follow the Dinkumware 26-byte format with `EINVAL` for a buffer under 26 bytes, a null pointer or an out-of-range weekday/month; `ctime_s` uses the guest time zone of `localtime_s`, and the console's exact checks are not verified
- [__udivti3](../../core/libs/prx/libc/src/RuntimeSupport.cpp) (libc, re-exported by libSceAmpr) - the hardware traps on a zero divisor; this throws instead
- [__umodti3](../../core/libs/prx/libc/src/RuntimeSupport.cpp) (libc) - the hardware traps on a zero divisor; this throws instead
- [AC1FtjqMCL0](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Unimplemented.cpp) (libkernel) - unknown name, signature
- [0iEKNAvT600](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Unimplemented.cpp) (libkernel) - unknown name, signature
- [2cELEBPdYQ0](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Unimplemented.cpp) (libkernel) - unknown name, signature
- [5xYxiOtOccA](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Unimplemented.cpp) (libkernel) - unknown name, signature
- [6W+shIH315Q](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Unimplemented.cpp) (libkernel) - unknown name, signature
- [7ilObq815O4](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Unimplemented.cpp) (libkernel) - unknown name, signature
- [7iq7Hfs8HBk](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Unimplemented.cpp) (libkernel) - unknown name, signature
- [AMJZhpC+siY](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Unimplemented.cpp) (libkernel) - unknown name, signature
- [E9XkHowt+dY](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Unimplemented.cpp) (libkernel) - unknown name, signature
- [GADjreszOgM](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Unimplemented.cpp) (libkernel) - unknown name, signature
- [Giyg2xGrXDU](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Unimplemented.cpp) (libkernel) - unknown name, signature
- [KC3y21wrqzc](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Unimplemented.cpp) (libkernel) - unknown name, signature
- [Qb8wS4GZUsw](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Unimplemented.cpp) (libkernel) - unknown name, signature
- [aAQZSjb2fho](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Unimplemented.cpp) (libkernel) - unknown name, signature
- [pnz21I6VjDs](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Unimplemented.cpp) (libkernel) - unknown name, signature
- [qk043QPsrZU](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Unimplemented.cpp) (libkernel) - unknown name, signature
- [uDGXwUKqi8s](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Unimplemented.cpp) (libkernel) - unknown name, signature
- [sceKernelGetCpuTemperature](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Unimplemented.cpp) (libkernel) - imported by PPSA99902, which fails to load without it; added without an implementation because its behavior is unverified.
- [LinuxProtFromSce](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/DirectMemory/DirectMemory.cpp) (libkernel) - protection bits 0x100 and 0x200 are accepted and assumed not to affect host CPU access; host protection is derived only from the read, write and execute bits
- [_sceUlobjmgrRegisterObject](../../core/libs/prx/ulobjmgr/Export.cpp) (ulobjmgr) - parameters taken from shadPS4 (PS4): a nonzero 64-bit object, a nonzero 32-bit kind and a 32-bit id output; the meaning of the first two and the PS5 signature are unverified. The id bound of `_sceUlobjmgrUnregisterObject` (below 0x4000) and the raw `EINVAL` (22) return also come from shadPS4
- [sceShareGetCurrentStatus](../../core/libs/prx/libSceShare/Export.cpp) (libSceShare) - validation (feature flag non-zero, status non-null) and the all-zero 16-byte status taken from the [KytyPS5](https://github.com/KytyPS5/KytyPS5) reimplementation, not confirmed on a PS5 title; the meaning of the recording status values is unknown
- [sceShareGetRunningStatus](../../core/libs/prx/libSceShare/Export.cpp) (libSceShare) - only the first 32-bit word of the status is known: PPSA01317 treats bits 0x3c of it as a running share feature and gives the call a 40-byte buffer. The host never records or broadcasts, so it stores 0 in that word and leaves the rest untouched; a null status returns `INVALID_PARAM` by analogy with `sceShareGetCurrentStatus`
- [sceShareSetContentParamForApplicationTitle](../../core/libs/prx/libSceShare/Export.cpp) (libSceShare) - imported by PPSA12544's Unity plugins; the signature follows [KytyPS5](https://github.com/KytyPS5/KytyPS5/blob/main/src/libs/libShare.cpp), not confirmed on a PS5 title. The application title is only checked for null, as KytyPS5 does, since nothing is captured
- [sceSharePlayInitialize](../../core/libs/prx/libSceSharePlay/Export.cpp) (libSceSharePlay) - the library keeps no state, so the heap is not used. A null heap is accepted: PPSA11386 passes a null heap and 0x1800 bytes and stops on any non-zero result. The error for a zero size is unknown, so it throws
- [sceAgcDcbSetZPassPredicationEnableGetSize, sceAgcDcbSetPredicationDisableGetSize, sceAgcDcbSetBoolPredicationEnableGetSize](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgc/DcbState/src/Predication.cpp) (libSceAgc) - the builders are inline in the SDK and not exported; the size is assumed to be that of the SET_PREDICATION packet `sceAgcDcbSetPredication` writes (16 bytes)
- [Resource registration](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Resource/src/Registration.cpp) (libSceAgcDriver) - registration always fails with `RESOURCE_REGISTRATION_UNAVAILABLE`, so the owner and resource queries, `sceAgcDriverSetResourceUserData`, `sceAgcDriverFindResourcesPublic` and `sceAgcDriverUnregisterAllResourcesForOwner` return the same error without reading their arguments. Their parameter lists follow the libSceGnmDriver counterparts and are unverified. `sceAgcDriverGetResourceRegistrationMaxNameLength` writes 0xfc and returns 0, as [prosper](https://github.com/mattias800/prosper) does from a title's registration configuration; the `uint32_t*` parameter matches the SDK wrapper `sce::Agc::ResourceRegistration::getMaxNameLength(unsigned int*)`. sharpemu writes 256 and KytyPS5 returns `RESOURCE_REGISTRATION_UNAVAILABLE`, so the value is unverified; a null output throws
- [sceAgcDriverRegisterMultipleResources](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Resource/src/Registration.cpp) uses the existing unavailable-registration state and leaves caller memory untouched. Its four arguments (uint32 handle output, uint32 owner, descriptor pointer, uint32 count) follow the original PPSA34502 / 01.010.000 codzm call. The caller builds 40-byte descriptors and explicitly accepts 0x8A6C9018 with an invalid output handle. Enabled registration and console argument-validation precedence are unverified.
- [Shader registration](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Shaders/ShaderRegistry.cpp) (libSceAgcDriver) - a shader header is accepted at any address: PPSA26344 passes headers 4 and 1 bytes past an 8-byte boundary; whether the console refuses an alignment is unknown
- [Shader header copies](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Shaders/ShaderRegistry.cpp) (libSceAgcDriver) - a header bound for a draw or dispatch at another address than the registered one is accepted when its fixed fields match the registered header except `user_data`; the registered header's metadata is used. PPSA21567 binds such a copy of a vertex shader header with `user_data` set to null. Whether the console reads the copy's `user_data` is unknown
- [HtileFillCovers](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/DepthSurface.cpp) (libSceAgcDriver) - a fill is taken to cover a depth surface's whole HTILE when it spans 32 KiB per 1024x512 pixel block from DB_HTILE_DATA_BASE, the pipe-aligned SW_64KB_Z_X metadata extent AMD PAL's gfx10 addrlib gives for GB_ADDR_CONFIG 0x00100044 (1-8 samples, D16/D32); other metadata layouts are not decoded. A partial fill is ignored (the surface keeps its contents); not measured on the console.
- [Depth fast clear through HTILE](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/DepthSurface.cpp) (libSceAgcDriver) - a surface cleared by an HTILE fill is cleared to DB_DEPTH_CLEAR, also when it is next sampled. For a depth-only HTILE the clear word also carries a value (radv writes `minz = maxz = round(depth * 0x3fff)`, `HTILE_Z_CLEAR_REG` in Mesa's `src/amd/common/ac_descriptors.h`, and allows TC-compatible fast clears only to 0.0 or 1.0); which of the two the texture unit returns for a cleared tile was not measured.
- [Depth-only and stencil HTILE fill](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/DepthSurface.cpp) (libSceAgcDriver) - in a whole-HTILE fill, ZMask (bits 0-3) 0 clears the depth and SMem (bits 8-9) 0 clears the stencil, while SMem 3 leaves the stencil alone (`HtileFillClears`); other SMem values are taken to leave it alone too. The bit meanings follow the gfx10 HTILE layout as the code reads it, not a measurement; `agc_driver_depth_fast_clear_submit` pins them for the paths the Driver takes.
- [HTILE address of a depth array slice](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/State.cpp) (libSceAgcDriver) - `decodeDepth` adds the `DB_DEPTH_VIEW` slice offset to the Z and stencil addresses but not to `htileAddress`, so every slice of a layered depth target shares slice 0's HTILE address and a fill of slice 0's HTILE marks all slices cleared. Astro's Playroom fills the HTILE of each of its three shadow cascades 0x40000 apart (0x24000 bytes of tiles each) back to back before drawing, so the result is still right there; a title that clears one slice between draws to another would lose depth. The per-slice HTILE stride is not derived yet.
- [sceNgs2VoiceControl sampler filter](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNgs2.native/src/Voice.cpp) (libSceNgs2.native) - param `0x1000000a`, its layout and meaning follow KytyPS5 only: location 1 type 1 is an RBJ low-pass biquad at the system sample rate after resampling, scaled by `level`, a set `channel_mask` bit bypasses that channel, type 0 turns the filter off. Other locations and types throw. The voice keeps playing a filter tail after its last block until the history is zero, with outputs below 1e-20 flushed to zero (a KytyPS5 choice); the console's tail length is unknown
- [sceNgs2PanInit / sceNgs2PanGetVolumeMatrix](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNgs2.native/src/Pan.cpp) (libSceNgs2.native) - follow the PS4 firmware 11.00 `libSceNgs2.sprx`, read and checked against its output for 2, 4, 5 and 7 speakers into 1, 2, 6 and 8 channels; not yet confirmed on the PS5 library. Speakers are taken in the order L R, L R Ls Rs, L R C Ls Rs or L R C Ls Rs Lb Rb and kept in a fixed circular order, not sorted; without angles the defaults are 90/270, 40/110/250/320, 0/40/110/250/320 and 0/40/110/150/210/250/320 degrees. As in that library, defaults converted to radians keep the wrap-around entry in degrees, so angles past the last speaker pan almost fully to it. Error codes are not known for the PS5, so invalid arguments throw, including a speaker angle equal to the unit angle, on which that library loops forever. Angles so large that adding or subtracting a turn leaves them unchanged throw for the same reason
- [sceRtcGetDosTime, sceRtcSetDosTime](../../core/libs/prx/libSceRtc/Export.cpp) (libSceRtc) - bit layout from shadPS4 (PS4, the standard FAT date/time). A year outside 1980-2107 makes `GetDosTime` return `INVALID_YEAR` after writing 0 for an earlier one and `0xff9fbf7d` (2107-12-31 23:59:58, the largest encodable time) for a later one, and `SetDosTime` decodes the year as the unsigned 7-bit FAT field (1980-2107) without validating the fields, as the disassembly-derived [fpPS4](https://github.com/red-prig/fpPS4) reimplementation of the PS4 library does; not confirmed on a PS5 title, and shadPS4 instead masks out-of-range years into the packed value and sign-extends the 16-bit date
- [sce::Json::InitParameter2](../../core/libs/prx/libSceJson2/Export.cpp) (libSceJson2) - layout inferred, not known: assumed to start with the allocator, its user data and the file buffer size, in that order, and the whole struct is assumed to fit in 40 bytes; `Initializer::initialize(InitParameter2)` ignores them as `initialize(InitParameter)` does
- [sceAjmBatchJobSetResampleParameters, sceAjmBatchJobGetResampleInfo](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAjm.native/src/Ajm.cpp) (libSceAjm) - the ratio is taken as decoded samples per output sample and num_samples as the decoded samples the resampler holds, both inferred from FMOD (Hades II); the console's interpolation is unknown, a 4-point Catmull-Rom is used. The flags argument is ignored, as in shadPS4 and Kyty
- [scePlayerReviewDialogOpen, scePlayerReviewDialogGetResult](../../core/libs/prx/libSceCdlgPlayerReview/Export.cpp) (libSceCdlgPlayerReview) - the PS5-only dialog has no public reference: the parameter is not read and the result is assumed to start with the 32-bit common dialog result, as `sceWebBrowserDialogGetResult` assumes
- [sceHttpWaitRequest](../../core/libs/prx/libSceHttp/Export.cpp) (libSceHttp) - the timeout is assumed to be in microseconds and a negative timeout to wait without limit; the invalid-value error for a null handle or event array or a non-positive event count is assumed, not measured. No request is ever pending, so the call waits for the timeout and returns 0 events, and a wait without limit throws
- [sceVideoOutAddPreVblankStartEvent](../../core/libs/prx/libSceVideoOut/src/Event.cpp) (libSceVideoOut) - how long before the vblank the console signals pre-vblank start is unknown. The event is triggered on each emulated vblank tick immediately before the vblank event, with its own count as the event data
- [sched_get_priority_max, sched_get_priority_min](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/System/src/Process.cpp) (libkernel) - measured on a PS5 (firmware 13.60): `SCHED_FIFO` (1) and `SCHED_RR` (3) report a maximum of 767 and a minimum of 256, and every other policy, `SCHED_OTHER` (2) included, returns -1 with `EINVAL`. `pthread_setschedparam` and `scePthreadSetschedparam` accept those two policies with a priority of 256 to 767 or 896 to 959 and keep the policy, as measured; a created thread takes the policy and priority of explicit scheduling attributes, or its creator's when it inherits, as it does with a null attribute
- [sceSystemServiceLoadExec](../../core/libs/prx/libSceSystemService/Export.cpp) (libSceSystemService) - `"exit"` runs the guest shutdown callbacks and then ends the process without running the guest's `atexit` or `__cxa_atexit` handlers, as an exit from the system service does. Only the title's own `/app0/eboot.bin` without arguments is relaunched; another executable or arguments throw
- [sceKernelConvertUtcToLocaltime, sceKernelConvertLocaltimeToUtc](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Time/Time.cpp) (libkernel) convert with the host time zone at the given instant, as libc `localtime` does, instead of the console's time zone setting. `west_sec` holds the standard-time offset in seconds east of UTC, despite its name, and `dst_sec` the daylight saving seconds in effect. This follows a homebrew emulator ([PS5SX2](https://github.com/Swordpdf/PS5SX2/blob/b4cf55e87c34e8a2a1cd9302df4373e5a94d28f4/ps5/frontend/fe_ps5.cpp#L54-L63)), whose author saw a console write 7200 and 3600 into the 16-byte block; which zone that console was set to is not recorded, so whether `west_sec` includes daylight saving is not settled. [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/blob/23c41333/src/core/libraries/kernel/time.cpp) also stores seconds east. That emulator declares the last argument of `sceKernelConvertUtcToLocaltime` as `uint64_t*`; 32 bits are written. On Windows the standard offset and the daylight saving amount are the C runtime's current `_get_timezone` and `_get_dstbias` for every instant, as libc `localtime_s` uses them, so instants under a zone's earlier rules get today's offsets. In a repeated or skipped local hour, `sceKernelConvertLocaltimeToUtc` takes the offset in effect one standard offset earlier. Its last argument receives the zone's daylight saving amount whether or not it is in effect, found by sampling the host zone over the year around the result, while `dst_sec` is the amount in effect: the libc `mktime` that a homebrew title bundles subtracts that amount when `tm_isdst` is set outside summer time. That `mktime` passes `tm_isdst` as the second argument, which is ignored, so it does not choose the offset in a repeated hour. `sceKernelGettimezone` and the `gettimeofday` time zone still report no offset and no daylight saving. A host time the C runtime cannot convert throws

- [gethostbyname](../../core/libs/prx/libScePosixForWebKit/Resolver.cpp) (libScePosixForWebKit) - numeric IPv4 forms, trailing whitespace and 64-bit accumulation follow FreeBSD 11.4 [inet_aton](https://github.com/freebsd/freebsd-src/blob/releng/11.4/lib/libc/inet/inet_addr.c); clearing the resolver error on numeric success follows [fakeaddr](https://github.com/freebsd/freebsd-src/blob/releng/11.4/lib/libc/net/gethostnamadr.c). Exact PS5 behavior is unverified; hostname lookup still uses the host resolver

- [sceAppContentGetAddcontDownloadProgress](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAppContent/Export.cpp) (libSceAppContent) - imported by PPSA08755, which fails to load without it; added without an implementation because its behavior is unverified. [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero) lists `(uint32_t serviceLabel, entitlementLabel, SceAppContentAddcontDownloadProgress*)`, but no PS5 reference gives the progress layout or the error codes
- [sceNgs2GeomResetListenerParam, sceNgs2GeomResetSourceParam](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNgs2.native/src/Geom.cpp) (libSceNgs2.native) - the structures (12, 16, 16, 60, 96, 108, 32 and 308 bytes) come from [KytyPS5](https://github.com/KytyPS5/KytyPS5)'s `ngs2.cpp` and match [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4)'s `ngs2_geom.h` field for field, but only KytyPS5 gives the behaviour - shadPS4 logs and returns success without writing the output. The defaults (orient front +Z, orient up +Y, sound speed 343, cone 1/360/1/360, rolloff 0/1000000/1/1, doppler factor, full-band, LFE and maximum level 1, two speakers and matrix format 2) are unverified on a PS5 title; KytyPS5 and iStark/PS5PCEM agree on them. PPSA15552 (Dead Cells) calls `sceNgs2GeomResetSourceParam` after creating its racks. A null output address returns `INVALID_OUT_ADDRESS` as elsewhere in the library rather than throwing as KytyPS5 does. `sceNgs2GeomApply` stays unimplemented
- [sceNgs2GeomCalcListener](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNgs2.native/src/Geom.cpp) builds an orthonormal world-to-listener transform. The row-major matrix, front-axis priority and flag 1 for right-handed input follow [PS5PCEM](https://github.com/iStark/PS5PCEM/blob/d2534bac6325a2c61c0fa88977e5321074304484/src/hle/ngs2_geom.zig); they have not been measured on PS5 hardware. Velocity stays in world coordinates and positive finite sound speed is preserved. Orientation vectors are normalised and up is made perpendicular to front, so non-unit and slightly tilted vectors are accepted; unknown flags, zero or parallel orientation vectors, nonfinite input and an unrepresentable translation throw without changing the output; console error codes are unknown, and a nonpositive sound speed throws where PS5PCEM and [KytyPS5](https://github.com/KytyPS5/KytyPS5/blob/df5b9b92be7dfd463b47756b707d6d356a9f0550/src/libs/ngs2.cpp) default it to 343. KytyPS5 always writes the identity matrix, so the transform rests on PS5PCEM alone. Dead Cells (PPSA15552) reaches this call after the custom sampler rack in #4334. This does not implement source attenuation, panning or Doppler in `sceNgs2GeomApply`.

- [_sceFiberGetThreadFramePointerAddress](../../core/libs/prx/libSceFiber/Export.cpp) (libSceFiber, NID `0dy4JtMUcMQ`) forwards to `sceFiberGetThreadFramePointerAddress`; that the argument list matches is inferred from the export without the underscore, as for the underscore Ult exports, not measured

- [sceAgcAcbWaitUntilSafeForRendering](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgc/Acb/src/Control.cpp) (libSceAgc) - `(buffer, videoOutHandle, displayBufferIndex)`, the declared arguments, which PPSA12544 (01.000.010) passes inside `waitUntilSafeForRendering` and `waitUntilSafeForRendering VR` markers on an asynchronous compute command buffer, ignoring the result. It writes the same rendering-wait packet as `sceAgcDcbWaitUntilSafeForRendering`, which the driver now accepts on compute queues too; the wait blocks only that queue's worker. Whether the console encodes the compute variant differently is not known
- [sceSslCreateConnection, sceSslDeleteConnection, sceSslSetVerifyCallback, sceSslConnect, sceSslRead, sceSslWrite](../../core/libs/prx/libSceSsl/Export.cpp) (libSceSsl) - imported by `ffmpeg.prx`. The repository has no TLS client, so a connection only records its context, socket, host and verification callback, and `sceSslConnect`, `sceSslRead` and `sceSslWrite` throw `NotImplemented_nid_no_patch(__func__)` until there is a transport. Signatures follow [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero/blob/60977b29103452b9681ae10c7a9011d44965bf16/src/SharpProspero/Interop/Net/Http.cs#L195-L252), which also says the socket stays open after `sceSslDeleteConnection`. An unknown context returns `SSL_ERROR_INVALID_ID` (0x8095F006), as `sceSslGetMemoryPoolStats` does on the console and as [KytyPS5](https://github.com/KytyPS5/KytyPS5/blob/4865dd92ca6b6bb907748f125883093af038f53b/src/libs/network.cpp#L2688-L2720) and [sharpemu](https://github.com/sharpemu/sharpemu/blob/29021b5a71bbc80f262d887e6ba18b3a72963f49/src/SharpEmu.Libs/Network/SslExports.cs#L41-L54) return for other context calls; for an unknown connection, a connection whose context was terminated and a null host the console's codes are unknown, so these throw. The socket is not checked when the connection is created, `sceSslTerm` leaves the context's connections to `sceSslDeleteConnection`, and `sceSslSetVerifyCallback` accepts any callback, null included, and returns 0; none of this is verified on the console. `SSL_ERROR_BEFORE_INIT` (0x8095F001) is never returned, as the library keeps no state apart from its contexts
- [sceNgs2VoiceGetState custom sampler](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNgs2.native/src/Voice.cpp) (libSceNgs2.native, rack 0x4001) - only the size and one field come from a title. One tested title calls it on custom sampler voices with a 48-byte buffer at two sites (eboot `0x1740f90`, `0x1741009`) and reads only the 64-bit value at offset 16: the first site keeps it and the second subtracts the kept value, so it is filled with the voice's decoded sample count. Other sizes return `INVALID_OUT_SIZE` as for the other racks. The state flags fill the common voice state; the remaining fields stay zero. shadPS4's `OrbisNgs2CustomSamplerVoiceState` is about 80 bytes and does not match the size this title passes, so the field names and the console's value are unverified
- [sceCesRefersUcsProfileCp1250](../../core/libs/prx/libSceCesCs/Export.cpp) (libSceCesCs) - declared without parameters and returning a pointer, following `sceCesRefersUcsProfileCp1251`; the signature and behavior are unverified. One tested title fails to start without this export (`qYu6y1E0Qbg`).
- Two tested titles' imports declared without parameters, signatures unknown, added without an implementation because the titles fail to load without them: [sceNetShowIfconfig](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNet/Export.cpp) (libSceNet) and [scePadUnknown_n3kSX62fgNo](../../core/libs/prx/libScePad/Export.cpp) (libScePad), whose real name is unknown. [_sceUltWaitingQueueResourcePoolCreate, _sceUltSemaphoreCreate, _sceUltMutexOptParamInitialize, _sceUltMutexCreate, _sceUltQueueDataResourcePoolCreate and _sceUltQueueCreate](../../core/libs/prx/libSceUlt/Export.cpp) (libSceUlt) forward to the exports without the underscore; that their argument lists match is inferred from the other underscore Ult exports, not measured.
- [sceVideoOutGetBufferLabelAddress](../../core/libs/prx/libSceVideoOut/src/Buffer.cpp) exposes a port-owned contiguous array of 16 aligned 64-bit labels and returns its count. A title's bundled AGC driver passes a pointer output and addresses each label at `base + index * 8`. The count, zero initialization on registration, and release of the previous displayed buffer when its replacement completes follow [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/blob/23c41333822fe8eca4c609b43119180af63ae4ac/src/core/libraries/videoout/video_out.cpp) and its driver; PS5 console equivalence has not been measured. Labels live until the port closes, and reopening creates a new array. Error precedence follows the reference for null output and the existing open-port policy for handles. Exact scanout release timing, repeated same-buffer flips and concurrent guest label stores remain unverified. EOP-flip registration and interrupt tokens remain unimplemented; exposing these labels does not implement that separate API.
- [sceImeOpen, sceImeUpdate, sceImeSetText, sceImeSetCaret, sceImeClose](../../core/libs/prx/libSceIme/Export.cpp) (libSceIme) - the event ids (Open 0, UpdateText 1, UpdateCaret 2, PressClose 4, PressEnter 5), the caret directions (left 1, right 2), the error codes and the text event payload follow the PS4 reimplementation in [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/blob/b72489d8c24e63fc56a29fe2c90120ab768a210d/src/core/libraries/ime/ime.cpp); none is measured on a PS5. Events are queued and delivered by `sceImeUpdate` only when it is passed the handler given to `sceImeOpen`, with that call's `arg`. A text event points `str` at a copy of the text kept in `work`, and `inputTextBuffer` holds the same text; both are terminated, `caret_index` is the caret after the edit and the one edit area has the edit position and the change in UTF-16 units (0 for Enter and Close). An edit sends one UpdateText; a caret move sends one UpdateCaret per UTF-16 unit, as shadPS4 does. Enter inserts a line break with the multiline option and sends PressEnter otherwise; Escape sends PressClose. Typed text is cut to `maxTextLength` without splitting a surrogate pair, line breaks are dropped without the multiline option, and the BasicLatin, URL, mail and number types do not filter characters. `sceImeOpen` checks, in shadPS4's order, the user, type, enter label, input method, option bits (the `sceImeGetPanelSize` mask), `maxTextLength` (1 to 2048), the text buffer, the position (below 1920x1080, or 3840x2160 with the over-2K option), the alignments, the extended priority and reserved bytes, `work` (non-null, 4-byte aligned), the handler, the reserved bytes and the initial text (no unpaired surrogate, no line break without the multiline option); shadPS4's SDK-dependent option, language and extended-option masks and its work-overlap check are not applied, and only the text copy is written to `work`. A text filter or an external keyboard filter throws, as their contract is unknown. `sceImeSetText` keeps the caret unless the text became shorter, `sceImeSetCaret` rejects an index past the text with `0x80bc0030`, and neither sends an event. `sceImeSetTextGeometry` accepts any geometry while a panel is open, since no panel is drawn
- [libSceJpegDec](../../core/libs/prx/libSceJpegDec/Export.cpp) (libSceJpegDec) - decode modes other than 0 and down scales other than 1 throw

### Functional

- [pthread_mutexattr_getpshared and pthread_mutexattr_setpshared](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Pthread/Posix/Mutex.cpp) (libkernel) store and report private or shared attributes, following FreeBSD 11 libthr [thr_mutexattr.c](https://github.com/freebsd/freebsd-src/blob/stable/11/lib/libthr/thread/thr_mutexattr.c). Invalid values return `EINVAL` without changing the attribute. Guest threads execute in one host process; sharing mutexes across processes is not modeled. Console behavior and invalid-attribute error precedence are unverified.

- [sceAudioInHqOpen, sceAudioInAsyncOpen](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAudioIn/Export.cpp) (libSceAudioIn) open the same port as `sceAudioInOpen`, so `sceAudioInInput`, `sceAudioInGetSilentState` and `sceAudioInClose` accept their handles. The signatures agree across [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/blob/fc5d2cc219c43c8c8f354153e7b3d0c04a5d506e/src/core/libraries/audio/audioin.cpp), [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero/blob/60977b29103452b9681ae10c7a9011d44965bf16/src/SharpProspero/Interop/Audio/AudioIn.cs) and [KytyPS5](https://github.com/KytyPS5/KytyPS5/blob/c86c8b4d9333a766cc8c4495f89f893bf35e6199/src/libs/audio.cpp). HqOpen is limited to 128 samples at 48 kHz; AsyncOpen rejects sizes above 384 and throws for currently unverified inputs. Host capture pacing and console behavior remain unverified.

- [sem_timedwait](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Pthread/Posix/Sem.cpp) (libkernel) consumes an available count before checking the timeout and waits indefinitely for a null timeout, following FreeBSD 11's `sem_timedwait`. Console behavior for null and invalid timeouts when a count is available is unverified.
- [MaskedSelectEliminator](../../core/shader/recompiler/Optimization/src/MaskedSelectEliminator.cpp) can discard inactive source lanes through `ReadFirstLane` only when its read mask implies the write mask and its result is unobserved outside the read mask. A loop-local read mask requires result uses in the same block without phi transport. The empty-EXEC lane-zero fallback follows AMD's [Vega ISA, section 12.8, V_READFIRSTLANE_B32, page 127](https://www.amd.com/content/dam/amd/en/documents/radeon-tech-docs/instruction-set-architectures/vega-shader-instruction-set-architecture.pdf) and the existing SPIR-V emitter. The optimization is covered by synthetic IR tests but has not been compared with console hardware; a proof that needs a read mask of a kind that is already being solved is refused.
